- Normalize a sum=1; maintain P = a (float length n).

  - D = C_target - C.
  - Compute Δ via FFT division: P_hat = rfft(pad P length L), D_hat = rfft(pad D), EPS for P_hat small: division with regularization: Δ_hat = D_hat / (2 P_hat + eps). But real division? We want Δ of length n approximating D/P in convolution. The optimal Δ in L2 sense can be found by solving the normal equation:
(P^T P) Δ = P^T D, where P^T P is n×n matrix with entries h_{i-j} = ∑_{k} P_k P_{k+i-j}, and P^T D = cross-correlation. This is a Toeplitz matrix! We can apply via FFT O(log n) and use conjugate gradient with nonneg? This is exact finite least squares! Nice.

Let's derive. Linear convolution c = P * Δ of length 2n-1. In matrix form c_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} P_{max(0,k-n+1..)/?} Δ_{?}. P^T c entries d_j = ∑_{k} P_{k-j} Δ? More directly, P^T D is cross-correlation of length n: d_j = ∑_{k=0}^{2n-2} P_{k-j?} D_k with valid P. Matrix K_j = P_{?}. The normal matrix for linear convolution PΔ: K_{j,l} = ∑_k P_{k-j} P_{k-l} with valid indices = cross-correlation of P with itself at lag j-l? Specifically h_{j-l} = ∑_m P_m P_{m+(j-l)} (valid 0..n-1). This is autocorrelation length 2n-1, Toeplitz. Thus we can solve K Δ = B where B_j = ∑_k P_{k-j} D_k? Let's derive MVD for K: For vector v length n, (K v)_j = ∑_{l=0}^{n-1} ∑_k P_k P_{k + j - l?} v_l. This is convolution/correlation. Let corr_p[q] = ∑_i P_i P_{i+q} for q=-(n-1)..n-1. Then (K v)_j = ∑_l h_{j-l} v_l where h_s = corr_p[-s]? This is convolution of v with h (length n). Can compute via FFT. Similarly B for D: B_j = ∑_k P_{?} D_k. If we define convolution matrix A (2n-1 × n) with (A v)_k = ∑_i P_i v_{k-i} (valid). A^T B? A^T D (n×2n-1) has entries B_j = ∑_k A_{k,j} D_k = ∑_k P_j? Let's compute A_{k,j}=P_j if 0≤k-j<n? Actually (P*Δ)_k=∑_j P_j Δ_{k-j}, so A_{k,j}=P_j if 0≤k-j≤n-1. Then (A^T D)_j=∑_k P_j D_{k-j} = P_j ∑_{t=0}^{k?} D_t? For j fixed, k-j ranges 0..n-1. So B_j=P_j * prefix/D sum over D[0..n-1]? Wait invalid range? If k from j to j+n-1, t=k-j from 0 to n-1. All within D length 2n-1. So B_j = P_j ∑_{t=0}^{n-1} D_t. That seems too simple? Let's test n=2: P=[p0,p1], Δ=[d0,d1], c0=p0 d0, c1=p0d1+p1d0, c2=p1d1. A = [[p0,0],[p0,p1],[0,p1]]. A^T D = [p0 D0 + p0 D1, p1 D1 + p1 D2] = [p0(D0+D1), p1(D1+D2)]. Formula P_j sum D[j:j+n]? j0 sum D0..D1 yes; j1 sum D1..D2 yes. Good. So B_j = P_j * sum_t D[t+j]? For j 0..n-1, t=0..n-1? yes D_{j+t} if exists. Since D length 2n-1, sum window length n starting at j. Nice.

Normal matrix K = A^T A. Entries K_{j,l}=∑_k P_j? A symmetric? K_{j,l}=P_j P_l if intervals [j,j+n-1] and [l,l+n-1] intersect? More exactly ∑_k A_{k,j}A_{k,l} = P_j P_l * ∑_{t=max(0,l-j)}^{n-1-|l-j|?} 1 = P_j P_l * (n - |j-l|) if |j-l|<n. Is that true? A_{k,j}=P_j if t=k-j∈[0,n-1]; A_{k,l}=P_l if k-l=s= t-(l-j) ∈[0,n-1]. Number of t satisfying both = n - |j-l|. Thus K = Toeplitz with diag n P_j^2, off diag (n-|j-l|)P_jP_l. This is simple! Much simpler than general. Wait A_{k,j}=P_j (not P_{?} because convolution P*Δ has P index fixed j, Δ index k-j, yes). This makes K = (P⊙P) * Toeplitz weights (n - |j-l|)?? K_{j,l} = P_j P_l * (n - |j-l|). Very nice.

Let's verify for n=3: A = [p0,0,0; p0,p1,0; p0,p1,p2; 0,p1,p2; 0,0,p2]; K00=p0*(1+1+1)=3p0^2; K01=p0*p1*(rows with both j0,l1: k=1,2 =>2); yes n-1. Good.

So finite least-squares Δ minimizing ∥P∘Δ - D∥^2 solves:

Algorithm `gn_flatten` pseudocode:
```
def gn_flatten(a, time_limit, max_iter, rng):
    a = normalize positive floor
    best = score
    tau = 0.0? target exponent
    for it:
       C = self_conv(a)
       maxC=C.max; sc=2n*maxC
       update best
       # target T
       Csm = C + eps
       if target_uniform: T = 1/(2n-1)
       else T = Csm**tau; T/=T.sum()
       # maybe blend with uniform: T = blend*uniform + (1-blend)*T
       E = 0.5*(T - C)
       # if sum residual? no
       S = sliding_window(E, n)
       u = solve_W(S, n)
       floor = max(1e-12, 1e-10/max(a))? Use P = np.maximum(a, eps)
       delta = u / P
       # maybe remove mean to avoid changing sum? Δ's effect on sum? P*Δ sum = ∑? Could add constant to P? Since scaling invariance, mean not issue. But to maintain positivity, maybe center delta: delta -= mean? Adding constant to delta changes P all; not affect low C sums? Could use.
       line_search alpha: candidates [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]
          cand = a + alpha*delta
          cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
          # If too negative, scale alpha smaller or use multiplicative clip
          cand = normalize(cand)
          sc = score(cand)
          if sc<best_sc: accept, a=cand,best=...
          else try smaller
       # if no improve, adjust target params (increase tau, reduce blend) or random perturbation
    return best
```
Need be careful: If delta large, a+alpha delta may negative. We can use multiplicative update: `cand = np.maximum(a * (1 + alpha * delta / a_floor), 0)`. Since delta can be negative, ratio may go negative if alpha delta < -a. The additive with line search may be enough. We can also project negativity by setting negative to 0 and maybe add small.

The exact division formula requires P positive; if a has zeros, floor. Floor should be small relative to a to not distort. Use `eps = 1e-10 * max(1, max(a))` maybe 1e-12. Since n~7000, after normalize values ~1e-4, floor 1e-12 negligible but division by it can create huge delta if u big. Need cap delta. Use `P = np.maximum(a, floor)` with floor maybe 1e-6? But if a near zero, changes matter. Use log-space update to avoid zeros? If a zero and u nonzero, desired Δ large to create support; but maybe okay with clipping. Use floor=1e-8 * sum/n? Maybe too high.

Let's examine scale: a_i~1/n≈1.4e-4. C~1/n≈1.4e-4. Difference T-C ~ few e-5. E ~1e-5. S window length n ~ maybe 0.01? def solve_W(z, n):
    if n==1: return np.array([z[0]])? W=[1]? For n=1, W_{0,0}=1, u=z.
    U = (z[0] + z[-1])/(n+1)
    d = np.diff(z) # length n-1, d[j]=z[j+1]-z[j]
    u=np.empty(n)
    u[0]=(U-d[0])/2
    if n>2: u[1:-1] = -(d[1:]-d[:-1])/2  # since u_j=(d_{j-1}-d_j)/2
    u[-1]=(U+d[-1])/2
    # Due to numerical, maybe update U? Could compute U sum u? maybe enforce by adding (Usum-U)/n? Should be exact.
```

Pseudo:
```
def gn_flatten(a_in, deadline=None, target_score=1.5, max_iter=100000, verbose=True):
    n=len(a_in); cn=2*n-1
    a = np.array(a_in, float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<0.01 return...
    a/=S

```
def water_target(C, L):
    cn=len(C)
    avg=1.0/cn
    if L <= avg: return np.full(cn, avg)
    if L >= float(C.max()) - 1e-15: return C.copy() # sum 1
    # binary ν
    cmin=float(C.min()); cmax=float(C.max())
    lo = -cmin  # all zeros maybe
    hi = max(L - cmin, cmax - cmin) # ensure sum>1
    # Actually sum at hi may not >1 if L low? But L>avg, sum at hi = cn*? If hi = L - cmin, for each C_i + hi >= L, clip=L, sum=cn*L>1. good.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       T=np.minimum(np.maximum(C+mid,0.0),L)
       s=float(T.sum())
       if s < 1.0: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    T=np.minimum(np.maximum(C+(lo+hi)/2,0.0),L)
    # adjust due rounding to sum 1 by tiny averaging? Could scale T to sum1 but may exceed L. Better iterative correction:
    # find correction eps small: if sumT !=1, adjust below L and above 0 by small epsilon with waterfill.
    diff=1 - T.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # apply small shift with same bounds, one or few binary iterations? Or add diff/cn to entries below L-eps, clip.
       # Since T sum close, just T += diff/cn; then clip to [0,L] and if sum off repeat few times.
       for _ in range(10):
           T = T + diff/cn
           T = np.clip(T,0,L)
           # if sum changed due clip, recompute
           diff = 1 - float(T.sum())
           if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
    return T
```

def random_perturb(a, rng, scale=0.01):
   n=len(a); noise=rng.standard_normal(n); cand=a+scale*noise; cand=np.maximum(cand,0); S=cand.sum(); cand/=S; return cand

def choose_lams(lam_max, max_abs_delta, max_a):
    if max_abs_delta==0: return np.array([0.0])
    lam_rel = 0.1 * max_a / max_abs_delta
    scale = min(1.0, lam_rel, 0.999*lam_max)
    if scale <=0: scale = 1e-12*lam_max if lam_max>0 else 0
    opts=[scale, scale*0.5, scale*0.25, scale*0.125, scale*0.0625, scale*0.03125, scale*0.015625]
    for f in [0.999,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
       opts.append(lam_max*f)
    opts.append(lam_max*1e-6)
    unique >0 <lam_max*1.001
```

Could there be a way to compute a better sequence analytically using polynomial square root of water target? Instead of iterative Gauss-Newton, we can initialize P as square root of target T via recursive coefficients. For a target C of length 2n-1, if we want P^2≈C, we can compute P coefficients recursively from first n C: a0=√c0, a_k=(c_k - ∑_{i=1}^k a_i a_{k-i})/(2a0) for k < n. This gives a polynomial P whose first n conv coeffs match C. Then score max of upper. This is like solving √C. For target waterfill, c0 maybe not zero. This could produce a good initial point. We can use it as initialization for flatten or as standalone. Let's explore.

Given target T length cn with T0>0. Define p0=sqrt(T0). For k=1..n-1:
`2 p0 p_k + ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} p_i p_{k-i} = T_k` ⇒ `p_k = (T_k - ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} p_i p_{k-i})/(2p0)`.
This is O(n^2) if direct; n=7000 =>49M multiplications, acceptable in Python? 49M ~ maybe 1-2s if vectorized? Could do using FFT via iterative? There is O(n log n) polynomial square root but complex. O(n^2) maybe okay for one or few targets, but not many. But we can implement O(n^2) in loops with NumPy dot? For n=7000, 25M average, `for k` with dot of length k -> 49/2=24.5M operations; Python weighted? NumPy vectorized each loop overhead 7000* small, maybe 0.5s-2s. Could be okay for initialization. Let's test mentally: 7000 iterations, each dot average 3500 floats => 24.5M multiply-add in C, overhead 7000 calls ~low. Could be ~0.1-0.5s. Fine.

Detailed `gn_flatten` implementation:
```
def gn_flatten(a_in, deadline=None, target_score=1.5, max_iter=100000, verbose=True):
    n = len(a_in)
    cn = 2*n - 1
    a = np.array(a_in, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12:
       a = np.ones(n)/n
       S=1.0
    else:
       a /= S
    C = self_conv(a)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = float(2.0*n*np.max(C))
    target_sc = float(target_score)
    if not np.isfinite(target_sc) or target_sc <= 0: target_sc = best_sc
    relax = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    small_floor = 1e-15 * max(1.0, float(np.max(a)))
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       # ensure positivity floor for solver
       if it % 50 == 0:
           a = np.maximum(a, small_floor); a/=a.sum()
           C = self_conv(a)
       m = float(np.max(C))
       sc = float(2.0*n*m)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       avg = 1.0/cn
       if target_sc < sc:
           m_target = target_sc/(2.0*n)
       else:
           m_target = m - 1e-7 * max(1e-300, m - avg)
       if m_target <= avg:
           L = avg + 1e-12*max(1e-300, m-avg)
       else:
           L = m_target
       if L >= m*(1-1e-12):
           L = m - 1e-12*max(1e-300,m-avg)
       # if target too aggr? relax
       if m > avg:
          f_target = max(0.0, (L - avg)/(m - avg))
       else: f_target=1.0
       f_use = 1.0 - relax * (1.0 - f_target)
       f_use = min(1.0, max(0.01, f_use))
       L_use = avg + f_use*(m-avg)
       T = water_target(C, L_use)
       c_sum = float(C.sum())
       T *= c_sum
       D = T - C
       dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
       if dmax < 1e-16 * max(1.0, m):
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 20: break
           continue
       E = 0.5 * D
       delta = solve_p_delta(a, E)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 10: break
           continue
       max_abs_delta = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
       if max_abs_delta <= 0:
           no_improve +=1; continue
       cap = 1e8 * max(1.0, float(np.max(a))) # if max a 0.01 cap1e6? 1e8*0.01=1e6
       delta = np.clip(delta, -cap, cap)
       max_abs_delta = max(float(np.max(np.abs(delta))), 1e-300)
       V = cross_conv(a, delta)
       W = self_conv(delta)
       sum_delta = float(delta.sum())
       neg = delta < 0.0
       if np.any(neg):
           lam_max = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-delta[neg]))) * 0.999
       else:
           lam_max = 1e6
       if not np.isfinite(lam_max) or lam_max <= 0:
           lam_max = 1e-12
       lam_rel = 0.2 * float(np.max(a)) / max_abs_delta
       scale = min(1.0, lam_rel, 0.999*lam_max)
       if scale <= 0 or not np.isfinite(scale): scale = 1e-9*lam_max
       # candidates
       opts = []
       # around scale
       for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625, 0.0078125):
           lam = scale*f
           if lam > 0 and lam <= lam_max: opts.append(lam)
       # near max
       for f in (0.999, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
           lam = lam_max*f
           if lam > 0: opts.append(lam)
       # log spaced
       if lam_max > 1e-15:
          for e in np.linspace(np.log10(max(1e-15,lam_max*1e-8)), np.log10(lam_max*0.999), 12):
             lam=10**e; if 0<lam<lam_max: opts.append(lam)
       best_lam = 0.0; best_try_sc = sc
       best_snew = 1.0; best_maxC = m
       # unique sort
       # To avoid too many, maybe max 50
       for lam in sorted(set(opts)): # set of floats problematic? duplicates close. Use if lam not too close.
```

```
def generic_target(C, mode, param):
   cn=len(C); avg=1.0/cn
   if mode==0: # water, param=L
      return water_target(C, param)
   if mode==1: # power, param=tau
      eps=1e-15/max(1,C.max())
      T=np.maximum(C,eps)**param
      tsum=T.sum(); if tsum<=0: T=np.full(cn,avg)
      else: T/=tsum
      return T
   if mode==2: # uniform
      return np.full(cn, avg)
```

def make_target(C, mode, L=0.0, tau=0.5):
   cn=len(C); c_sum=C.sum()
   if mode == 0:
      T=water_target(C, L); return T*c_sum
   if mode == 1:
      Cmax=C.max(); eps=1e-15*max(1.0,Cmax)
      T=np.maximum(C, eps) ** tau
      tsum=T.sum()
      if tsum <=0: T=np.full(cn, c_sum/cn)
      else: T *= c_sum / tsum
      return T
   if mode == 2:
      return np.full(cn, c_sum/cn)
```

Let's define `smooth_obj_and_grad(x, beta, reg)`:
Need be careful `x` might be list? It's ndarray. No bool.
```
def smooth_obj_and_grad(x, beta, reg):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
       grad=np.zeros_like(a)
       return 1e10, grad
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    z = beta*(c - cmax)
    z -= z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z)
    ezsum = float(ez.sum())
    if ezsum <=0: p=np.full(len(c),1/len(c)); logsum=0
    else: p=ez/ezsum; logsum=np.log(ezsum)
    Lval = cmax + logsum/beta
    # corr via FFT
    m2=2*len(a)-1; M=1 << (m2-1).bit_length()
    RA=np.fft.rfft(a, M); P=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(RA)*P, M)[:len(a)]
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    score = 2.0*len(a)*Lval/S2 + reg*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    grad = 2.0*len(a)*(2.0*corr/S2 - 2.0*Lval/S3) + 2.0*reg*(S-1.0)
    return float(score), grad
```
Check gradient: Score = K L/S^2. K=2n. ∂L=2corr? L=∑p(c), p independent? In softmax, derivative LSE = ∑p_k ∂c_k = 2corr. Yes. Then gradient =K(2corr/S2 -2L/S3). Plus reg. Good.
`corr` from FFT as previous. Ensure p length m2. If beta=0? We won't.

Run lbfgs:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxiter=2000,deadline=None,reg=100.0):
    a0=np.clip(a0,0,1000).copy()
    S=a0.sum(); if S<1e-6: a0=np.ones(len(a0))/n
    else: a0/=S
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':30}
    try:
      res=minimize(smooth_obj_and_grad,a0,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,bounds=bounds,args=(beta,reg),options=opts)
      a_out=np.clip(res.x,0,1000)
    except Exception:
      a_out=a0
    S=a_out.sum(); if S>1e-12: a_out/=S
    sc=score_of(a_out)
    return sc,a_out
```
No time check inside minimize, only budget by maxiter. Might overrun if maxiter too high. Use deadlines? Minimize doesn't support callback top-level? We can use small maxiter. Or implement callback? Let's think about proving `solve_p_delta` derivation orientation. A matrix rows convolution `(P*δ)_k =∑_{j=0}^{n-1} P_j δ_{k-j}` if valid. In `cross_conv(P,δ)` via np.convolve, `out_k = ∑ P_i δ_{k-i}`. Same. `A_{k,j}=P_j`. Normal matrix K_{j,l}=∑_{k} A_{k,j}A_{k,l}=P_j P_l * number of k such that 0≤k-j<n and 0≤k-l<n. Let t=k-j: t∈[0,n-1], and k =j+t; need k-l = t - (l-j) ∈[0,n-1] => t ∈ [l-j, n-1-l+j]. Count n-|j-l| if |j-l|<n. Yes. B_j=∑_k A_{k,j} E_k = P_j ∑_{t=0}^{n-1} E_{j+t}. Good.

Solving Kδ=b: K=diag(P) W diag(P). b=P∘S. Let u=diag(P)δ? Kδ = diag(P) W diag(P) δ. Equation diag(P) W u = P∘S. For P>0 divide: W u = S. Good. δ=u/P. Perfect.

Let's implement:
```
def poly_sqrt_from_target(T):
    n=(len(T)+1)//2
    p=np.zeros(n)
    if T[0] <=0: return None
    p0=np.sqrt(float(T[0])); p[0]=p0
    for k in range(1,n):
       # sum i=1..k-1 p_i p_{k-i}
       if k>1:
           # dot p[1:k] with reversed p[:k-1]
           dot = float(np.dot(p[1:k], p[k-1:0:-1]))
       else: dot=0.0
       val = (float(T[k]) - dot)/(2.0*p0)
       p[k]=val
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: return None
    return p/s
```
Note if T coefficients have cn length; only first n-1 T needed. Good.

Use `poly_sqrt_from_target(T)` in initializations:
- uniform target: T=1/(2n-1)*ones. p as above (central binomial scaled). Score maybe high but positive.
- U-shape C? For an initial a, C=self_conv(a), p=poly_sqrt(C); this is a smoothing of a? If C nearly square, p close a.

def build_initializations(n, parent, rng):
    inits=[]
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/n
    if parent is not None:
       inits.append(resample_sequence(parent,n))
       # perturbed parent
       p=resample...; p*= (1+0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)); p=np.maximum(p,0); inits.append(normalize(p))
       # flipped parent? inits.append(resample parent[::-1])
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    inits.append(1.0 - 0.5*np.sin? no)
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.6*(2*x-1)**2) # U
    inits.append((1.0 + 0.4*np.cos(4*np.pi*x)))
    # random smooth
    noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
    # moving average window max(3,n//50)
    win=max(3, n//50)
    c=np.cumsum(noise, dtype=float)
    c[win:] -= c[:-win]
    noise=c/win
    noise=(noise-0.5)/ (max(np.std(noise),1e-12))
    inits.append(0.8 + 0.3*noise)
    # geometric high endpoint
    r=1.0 - 5.0/n
    inits.append((1-r)*np.power(r, np.arange(n)))
    # beta-like
    eps=1.0/n
    inits.append(np.power(x+eps, -0.2) * np.power(1-x+eps, -0.3))
    # half-normal sqrt uniform maybe O(n^2) if n<=8000
    if n <= 8000:
       T=np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
       p=poly_sqrt_from_target(T)
       if p is not None: inits.append(p)
       # sqrt of U-shape C? Maybe skip.
    # filter normalize nonneg sum>0
    out=[]
    for a in inits:
       a=np.asarray(a,float)
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s>0.01? Actually internal normalize; if s tiny skip
       a=a/s
       if a.max()<1000: out.append(a)
    return out
```

Let's implement water_target efficiently. The binary `np.clip(C+mid,0,L)` each iter. To avoid 60, maybe 35. Sum target. Then correction. Code:
```
def water_target(C, L):
   cn=len(C)
   avg=1.0/cn
   if L <= avg + 1e-15:
      return np.full(cn, avg)
   cmx=float(C.max())
   if L >= cmx - 1e-15:
      T=C.copy(); Tsum=T.sum(); if Tsum>0: T*=1/Tsum; return T
   cmin=float(C.min())
   lo = -cmin
   hi = max(L - cmin, cmx - cmin + 1.0)
   # ensure sum at hi >1: if L small but hi maybe. Could double hi until sum>1 (few)
   for _ in range(10):
      s_hi=float(np.clip(C+hi,0,L).sum())
      if s_hi > 1.0: break
      hi = hi*2 + 1.0
   for i in range(45):
      mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
      T=np.clip(C+mid,0,L)
      s=float(T.sum())
      if s < 1.0: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   nu=(lo+hi)*0.5
   T=np.clip(C+nu,0,L)
   s=float(T.sum())
   if s>0: T*=1.0/s
   # after scaling may slightly exceed L; clip and rescale
   T=np.clip(T,0,L)
   s=float(T.sum())
   if s>0: T*=1.0/s
   # if still sum off due L, clip loop few
   for _ in range(5):
      s=float(T.sum())
      if s <=0: break
      T*=1.0/s
      T=np.clip(T,0,L)
   return T
```
Scaling after clip may push above L; loop. Since target close, okay. If final sum off, return T normalized by clipping; D sum slight not zero. We can adjust `T *= c_sum` later and D sum may off tiny. For line search, D sum need not exactly zero for formula; score formula uses actual sum_delta. Target waterfill not critical. But if T sum not exactly c_sum, C linear sum change nonzero; target score might drift. We can later adjust T to sum c_sum by adding small constant to unconstrained? Simpler: after water_target, `T *= c_sum` (in make_target), even if max slightly >L. Fine.

Now, `solve_W` detailed:
```
def solve_W(S, n):
    S=np.asarray(S, dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
       return S.copy()
    U = (S[0] + S[-1]) / (n + 1)
    d = np.diff(S)
    u = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    u[0] = 0.5 * (U - d[0])
    if n > 2:
       u[1:-1] = 0.5 * (d[:-1] - d[1:])
    u[-1] = 0.5 * (U + d[-1])
    return u
```
Check for n=2: d diff length1; u[1:-1] empty; u[-1]=0.5(U+d0) where last index -1=1. Good.

```
def solve_p_delta(P,E):
   n=len(P); cn=2*n-1
   pref=np.empty(cn+1); pref[0]=0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(E)
   S=pref[n:]-pref[:cn-n+1]? For j=0..n-1, pref[j+n]-pref[j]. `pref[n:]` length n, `pref[:n]` length n. So S=pref[n:] - pref[:n]. Good.
   u=solve_W(S,n)
   maxP=float(np.max(P)); meanP=float(np.mean(P))
   ridge=1e-10*max(1e-300, meanP)
   den=P+ridge
   delta=u/den
   # cap:
   cap = 1e8 * max(1e-12, maxP)
   delta=np.nan_to_num(delta, nan=0.0, posinf=cap, neginf=-cap)
   return np.clip(delta, -cap, cap)
```

def gn_flatten(a_in, deadline=None, target_score=1.5, max_iter=2000000, verbose=False):
    n=len(a_in); cn=2*n-1
    a=normalize_sequence(a_in,1.0)
    C=self_conv(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=float(2*n*np.max(C))
    target_sc=float(target_score)
    lb_score=2.0*n/cn + 1e-9
    if target_sc < lb_score: target_sc=lb_score
    relax=1.0; trust=1.0; no_imp=0; flipped=False; congruent? 
    rng=np.random.default_rng(777)
    small_floor=1e-15*max(1.0, float(np.max(a)))
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       a=np.maximum(a, small_floor); S=a.sum(); if S<=1e-12: break; a=S? a=a/S
       C=self_conv(a)
       m=float(np.max(C)); sc=float(2*n*m)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); if verbose print
       if sc <= lb_score + 1e-7: break
       # choose L
       if target_sc < sc:

       else:

       if m_target <= avg: L=avg + 1e-12*max(1e-300,m-avg)

       else: L=m_target
       if m>avg:

          f_use=1.0 - relax*(1.0-f_target)

          L_use=avg + f_use*(m-avg)
       else: L_use=avg
       T=water_target(C,L_use)
       csum=float(C.sum()); T*=csum
       D=T-C
       dmax=float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
       if dmax < 1e-15*max(1.0,m):
           no_imp +=1
           if no_imp>30: break
           continue
       E=0.5*D
       delta=solve_p_delta(a,E)
       mad=float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
       if mad <=1e-300: no_imp+=1; continue
       V=cross_conv(a,delta); W=self_conv(delta)
       s_delta=float(delta.sum())
       maxP=float(np.max(a))
       neg=delta<0
       if np.any(neg):
           lam_pos = float(np.min(np.maximum(a,small_floor)[neg] / (-delta[neg])))
       else:
           lam_pos = 1e9
       scale_limit = 20.0*trust*maxP/mad if mad>0 else 1e9
       lam_max = min(lam_pos*0.999, scale_limit, 1e6)
       if lam_max <=0 or not finite: lam_max=1e-12
       # candidates
       lams=[]
       base_scale = min(1.0, 5.0*maxP/mad, 0.999*lam_max)
       if base_scale <=0: base_scale=1e-12*lam_max
       for f in (...): lams.append(base_scale*f)
       for f in (...): lams.append(lam_max*f)
       # log spaced
       if lam_max > 1e-300:
          lo_log = max(-30.0, np.log10(lam_max*1e-8))
          hi_log = np.log10(lam_max*0.999)
          if hi_log > lo_log:
             for lg in np.linspace(lo_log, hi_log, 12):
                lam=10**lg
                if 0<lam<lam_max: lams.append(lam)
       best_lam=0; best_sc_try=sc; best_snew=1.0
       for lam in lams:
          if lam<=0 or lam>=lam_max*1.001: continue
          snew=1.0+lam*s_delta
          if snew <= 1e-12: continue
          Ctry = C + (2.0*lam)*V + (lam*lam)*W
          maxC=float(np.max(Ctry))
          sc_try=2.0*n*maxC/(snew*snew)
          if sc_try < best_sc_try:
              best_sc_try=sc_try; best_lam=lam; best_snew=snew
       accept = best_lam >0 and best_sc_try < sc
       if accept:

          # due tiny negative
          if np.any(a_try < 0):
              a_try = np.maximum(a_try,0.0)
          Snew = float(a_try.sum())
          if Snew > 1e-12:

             best_triangle? no
             no_imp=0
             if best_lam >= 0.9*lam_max: trust=min(1e5, trust*3.0); relax=min(1.0,relax*1.2)
             else: relax unchanged? maybe increase small if best_lam >0.4*lam_max
          else: no_imp +=1; trust=1; relax*=0.8
       else:
          no_imp +=1; trust=1.0; relax *=0.75
          if relax<0.05: relax=0.05
          if no_imp > 20 and (no_imp % 20 ==0):

                z=rng.standard_normal(n)
                cand=a*np.exp(scale*z)
                S=cand.sum(); if S>0: cand/=S

                scand=score_of(cand)

def build_initializations(n, parent, rng):

`resample_sequence` if seq length0? Handle.
```
def resample_sequence(seq,n):
    arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel()
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
    if arr.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if arr.size==n: return arr/arr.sum() if sum>0 else ones
    xs=np.linspace(0,1,arr.size); xn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(xn,xs,arr)
    out=np.maximum(out,0); s=out.sum(); return out/s if s>0 else ones
```
If parent length1 and n large, np.interp with one point? Need xs size1. `np.linspace(0,1,1)` works? np.interp with xk length1? It requires xp length same; if len=1, returns constant? I think yes but maybe. Handle if arr.size==1: return full arr[0]/n? If sum >0. Good.

For k=2, p[k-1:0:-1]=p[1:0:-1] length1 p1, dot=p1*p1 correct (sum i=1 to1 p_i p_{k-i}=p1*p1). For k=1 empty. For k=3: p[1:3]=[p1,p2]; p[2:0:-1]=[p2,p1]; dot p1p2+p2p1 =2p1p2; sum i=1..2 p_i p_{3-i}=p1p2+p2p1 correct. Good. O(n^2) with dot each step. Could be slow in Python loops but okay for <=8000.

def optimize_length(n, current_best, parent, rng, deadline, target_score):
    # ensure time
    now=time.monotonic()
    if deadline is not None and now >= deadline:
       return current_best.copy() if ... , score_of(current_best)
    init_candidates=[]
    if current_best is not None and len(current_best)>0:
       a_res=resample_sequence(current_best,n)
       init_candidates.append((score_of(a_res), a_res))
    inits=build_initializations(n,parent,rng)
    # maybe limit inits to first 8 to avoid time
    for a in inits[:10]:
       sc=score_of(a)
       if sc < 1.9: init_candidates.append((sc,a))
    if not init_candidates: init_candidates=[(2.0, np.ones(n)/n)]
    init_candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    a0=init_candidates[0][1]
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_sc=init_candidates[0][0]
    # If phase_time large, run top1; if remaining after, top2? JS runs until deadline. Could use 70/30 multiple starts.
    # For safety, if top2 score < top1+0.05 and phase_time > 60, run top1 first 60%, top2 40%.
    phase_duration = deadline - now
    if phase_duration > 120 and len(init_candidates)>1 and (init_candidates[1][0] < best_sc + 0.05):
        end1 = now + 0.6*phase_duration
        a1,sc1=gn_flatten(a0, deadline=end1,target_score=target_score,verbose=False)
        if sc1<best_sc: best_sc=sc1; best_a=a1
        end2=deadline
        a2,sc2=gn_flatten(init_candidates[1][1], deadline=end2,target_score=target_score,verbose=False)
        if sc2<best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_a=a2
    else:
        a1,sc1=gn_flatten(a0, deadline=deadline,target_score=target_score,verbose=False)
        if sc1<best_sc: best_sc=sc1; best_a=a1
    return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def make_valid_output(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 0.01:
      a=np.full(1024,0.5)
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```

Define `score_of`:
```
def score_of(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=self_conv(a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*len(a)*m/(S*S))
```
`self_conv` of zero? fine.

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a,target=1.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   S=float(a.sum())
   if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.full(len(a) if len(a)>0 else 1, target/max(1,len(a)))? 
   return a/S*target
```

`gn_flatten` needs `avg` defined. Let's write exact code carefully.

```
def gn_flatten(a_in, deadline=None, target_score=1.5, max_iter=1000000, verbose=False):
    n = len(a_in)
    cn = 2*n - 1
    avg = 1.0/cn
    lb_score = 2.0*n/cn + 1e-9
    target_sc = float(target_score)
    if not np.isfinite(target_sc) or target_sc <= lb_score:
        target_sc = lb_score
    a = normalize_sequence(a_in, 1.0)
    C = self_conv(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(2.0*n*float(np.max(C)))
    if best_sc <= lb_score + 1e-7:
        return best_a, best_sc
    relax = 1.0
    trust = 1.0
    no_imp = 0
    flipped = False
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765)
    max_a0 = float(np.max(a)) if n>0 else 1.0
    small_floor = 1e-15 * max(1.0, max_a0)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       a = np.maximum(a, small_floor)
       S = float(a.sum())
       if S <= 1e-12: break
       a = a / S
       C = self_conv(a)
       m = float(np.max(C))
       sc = float(2.0*n*m)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc = sc; best_a = a.copy()
           if verbose: print(...)
       if sc <= lb_score + 1e-7: break
       if target_sc < sc:
           m_target = target_sc/(2.0*n)
       else:
           m_target = m - 1e-6*max(1e-300, m-avg)
       if m_target <= avg:
           L = avg + 1e-12*max(1e-300, m-avg)
       elif m_target >= m:
           L = m - 1e-12*max(1e-300, m-avg)
       else:
           L = m_target
       if m > avg:
           f_target = (L - avg) / (m - avg)
           f_target = min(1.0, max(0.0, f_target))
           f_use = 1.0 - relax*(1.0 - f_target)
           f_use = min(1.0, max(0.02, f_use))
           L_use = avg + f_use*(m-avg)
       else:
           L_use = avg
       T = water_target(C, L_use)
       csum = float(C.sum())
       T = T * csum
       D = T - C
       dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
       if dmax <= 1e-15*max(1.0, m):
           no_imp +=1
           if no_imp > 50: break
           continue
       E = 0.5*D
       delta = solve_p_delta(a, E)
       mad = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
       if mad <= 1e-300:
           no_imp +=1
           if no_imp>50: break
           continue
       V = cross_conv(a, delta)
       W = self_conv(delta)
       s_delta = float(delta.sum())
       maxP = float(np.max(a))
       neg = delta < 0.0
       if np.any(neg):
           lam_pos = float(np.min(np.maximum(a, small_floor)[neg] / (-delta[neg])))
       else:
           lam_pos = 1e9
       if maxP > 0 and mad > 0:
           scale_limit = 2.0 * trust * maxP / mad
       else:
           scale_limit = 1e9
       lam_max = min(lam_pos*0.999, scale_limit, 1e6)
       if not np.isfinite(lam_max) or lam_max <= 0.0:
           lam_max = 1e-12
       lams=[]
       base_scale = min(1.0, 0.5*maxP/mad if mad>0 else 1.0, 0.999*lam_max)
       if base_scale <=0 or not np.isfinite(base_scale): base_scale=1e-12*lam_max
       for f in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625,0.0078125):
           lams.append(base_scale*f)
       for f in (0.999,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
           lams.append(lam_max*f)
       if lam_max > 1e-300:
           lo_log = max(-30.0, float(np.log10(lam_max*1e-8)))
           hi_log = float(np.log10(lam_max*0.999))
           if hi_log > lo_log:
               for lg in np.linspace(lo_log, hi_log, 12):
                   lams.append(float(10.0**lg))
       best_lam = 0.0
       best_try = sc
       for lam in lams:
           if lam <=0.0 or lam >= lam_max*1.001: continue
           snew = 1.0 + lam*s_delta
           if snew <= 1e-12: continue
           Ctry = C + (2.0*lam)*V + (lam*lam)*W
           maxC = float(np.max(Ctry))
           sc_try = 2.0*n*maxC/(snew*snew)
           if sc_try < best_try:
               best_try = sc_try; best_lam = lam
       accept = (best_lam > 0.0) and (best_try < sc)
       if accept:
           a_try = a + best_lam*delta
           if np.any(a_try < 0.0):
               a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
           Snew = float(a_try.sum())
           if Snew > 1e-12:
               a = a_try / Snew
               no_imp = 0
               if best_lam >= 0.9*lam_max:
                   trust = min(1e5, trust*3.0)
                   relax = min(1.0, relax*1.15)
               elif best_lam < 0.2*lam_max:
                   trust = max(1.0, trust*0.7)
                   relax = max(0.05, relax*0.9)
               if verbose and (it < 3 or it % 20 == 0): print(...)
           else:
               no_imp +=1
       else:
           no_imp +=1
           trust = 1.0
           relax = max(0.05, relax*0.75)
           if no_imp > 20 and (no_imp % 20 == 0):
               improved_perturb=False
               for attempt in range(3):
                   scale = 0.01 + 0.01*attempt
                   z = rng.standard_normal(n)
                   cand = a * np.exp(scale*z)
                   S = float(cand.sum())
                   if S > 1e-12:
                       cand = cand / S
                       cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                       S = float(cand.sum())
                       if S > 1e-12:
                           cand = cand / S
                           scand = score_of(cand)
                           if scand < sc * 1.01:
                               a = cand
                               no_imp = 0
                               relax = 0.6
                               trust = 1.0
                               improved_perturb=True
                               break
               if no_imp > 100 and not flipped:
                   a = a[::-1].copy()
                   flipped = True
                   no_imp = 0
                   relax = 0.6
           if no_imp > 350:
               break
    # final recompute
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S>1e-12: a=a/S
    C=self_conv(a); sc=float(2.0*n*float(np.max(C)))
    if sc < best_sc:
       best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter=50, maxfun=200, reg=20.0):
    if minimize is None: return score_of(a0), a0.copy()
    n=len(a0)
    a0=normalize_sequence(a0,1.0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0) for _ in range(n)]
    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20,'disp':False}
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_obj_and_grad, a0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, args=(beta, reg), options=options)
       a_out=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       a_out=a0.copy()
    a_out=np.maximum(a_out,0); S=a_out.sum(); if S>0: a_out/=S
    return score_of(a_out), a_out
```
No closures.

But `smooth_obj_and_grad` uses `self_conv`, which for x with n large and beta may be expensive. maxfun 200.

if arr.size == n: ...
if arr.size == 1:
   val=float(arr[0]); out=np.full(n,val); s=out.sum(); if s>0: out/=s; return out
x_old=np.linspace(0,1,arr.size)
x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,arr)
```
Good.

`build_initializations`: When using FFT lowpass, `weights` and noise arrays length n; for n small, works. `np.fft.rfftfreq(n)`. For n=1, rfftfreq([0]), weight; smooth = noise. Fine. But we don't use n<2048.

`poly_sqrt_from_target` O(n^2). If T has T0 small, p0 small; division may overflow. We can if T0 <=1e-300 return None. If val not finite or p negative too many? It clips. After p sum, maybe. Use `if not np.isfinite(val): return None`. If p has many negatives, clipping may zero many; maybe still. Return if p.sum >0.

Now, in `build_initializations`, if `poly_sqrt_from_target` for uniform T, p positive. Also for `parent C` not used. Good.

Now, check `make_valid_output`: It normalizes to sum1, changing values. If best_a length 0, uniform. The sum of uniform 1024*0.5=512, then normalized to 1/1024? Wait code:

Now, final verify: If evaluated score not finite, fallback. If evaluated finite but >3 maybe maybe fallback? Could if internal bug; but if best is 2 and evaluate 2.0. If evaluate > best_score+0.2, maybe use uniform if uniform better. We can do:

```

We shouldn't output worse than known? The known parent 1.506. If eval says >2.5, likely bug; output uniform 2. If val finite 1.505, okay. We'll fallback only if not finite.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)**2)

The main method is a Gauss-Newton / water-filling iteration.  For a current
non-negative vector a, we choose a target convolution T whose coefficients are
as flat as possible (or have a prescribed maximum level).  We then solve a
finite least-squares linearization
    P * delta ~= 0.5 * (T - P*P)
exactly using the normal equations.  This least-squares direction can be solved
in O(n) because the convolution matrix A for (A delta)_k = sum_j a_j delta_{k-j}
has A^T A = diag(a) W diag(a), where W_{ij}=n-|i-j|, and W has a simple
O(n) inverse.  Line search is done exactly using
    (a+lambda delta)^2 = a^2 + 2 lambda (a*delta) + lambda^2 (delta^2).

The solver uses FFT convolutions, adaptive trust/relaxation, random
multiplicative escapes, optional length resampling, and a small bounded
L-BFGS polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import operator

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


def self_conv(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, m_fft)[:m]


def cross_conv(a, b):
    """Full linear convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m_fft = 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, m_fft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, m_fft)[:n]


def score_of(a):
    """Exact internal objective, matching evaluate_sequence for valid input."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * m / (S * S))


def normalize_sequence(a, target=1.0):
    """Clip non-negative and rescale to sum target."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        n = max(1, len(a))
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (target / S) * a


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Resample a nonnegative sequence to length n by linear interpolation."""
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == n:
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            return arr / s
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 1:
        val = float(arr[0])
        out = np.full(n, val, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            return out / s
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def solve_W(S, n):
    """
    Invert W u = S where W_{ij}=n-|i-j|.

    A stable O(n) inverse is:
      U = (S_0 + S_{n-1})/(n+1)
      d_j = S_{j+1} - S_j
      u_0 = (U - d_0)/2
      u_j = (d_{j-1} - d_j)/2, 1<=j<=n-2
      u_{n-1} = (U + d_{n-2})/2
    """
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return S.copy()
    U = (S[0] + S[-1]) / float(n + 1)
    d = np.diff(S)
    u = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u[0] = 0.5 * (U - d[0])
    if n > 2:
        u[1:-1] = 0.5 * (d[:-1] - d[1:])
    u[-1] = 0.5 * (U + d[-1])
    return u


def solve_p_delta(P, E):
    """
    Solve finite least squares: minimize ||P * delta - E||_2,
    where P and delta have length n and E has length 2n-1.

    Returns delta.  If some P_j are tiny, a small ridge is used.
    """
    P = np.asarray(P, dtype=np.float64)
    E = np.asarray(E, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(P)
    cn = 2 * n - 1

    pref = np.empty(cn + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    pref[1:] = np.cumsum(E)

    # S_j = sum_{t=0}^{n-1} E_{j+t}
    S = pref[n:] - pref[:n]
    u = solve_W(S, n)

    maxP = float(np.max(P)) if n else 0.0
    meanP = float(np.mean(P)) if n else 0.0
    ridge = 1e-10 * max(1e-300, meanP)
    den = P + ridge
    delta = u / den

    cap = 1e8 * max(1e-12, maxP)
    delta = np.nan_to_num(delta, nan=0.0, posinf=cap, neginf=-cap)
    return np.clip(delta, -cap, cap)


def water_target(C, L):
    """
    L2 projection of C onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=1, x_i <= L}.
    If L is below 1/len(C), returns the uniform vector as the closest feasible
    max-constrained vector.
    """
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    cn = len(C)
    avg = 1.0 / cn

    if L <= avg + 1e-15:
        return np.full(cn, avg, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(C))
    if L >= cmax - 1e-15:
        T = C.copy()
        s = float(T.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            T /= s
        return T

    cmin = float(np.min(C))
    lo = -cmin
    hi = max(L - cmin, cmax - cmin + 1.0)

    # Guarantee hi gives sum > 1.
    for _ in range(12):
        s_hi = float(np.clip(C + hi, 0.0, L).sum())
        if s_hi > 1.0:
            break
        hi = hi * 2.0 + 1.0

    for _ in range(35):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        T = np.clip(C + mid, 0.0, L)
        s = float(T.sum())
        if s < 1.0:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    nu = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    T = np.clip(C + nu, 0.0, L)
    s = float(T.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        T *= 1.0 / s

    # Clamp to bound and renormalize a few times to remove roundoff.
    for _ in range(8):
        T = np.clip(T, 0.0, L)
        s = float(T.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            break
        T *= 1.0 / s
    return T


def poly_sqrt_from_target(T):
    """
    Given target first-half convolution coefficients, reconstruct a non-negative
    approximate square root P whose first n convolution coefficients match T.

    If T[0] <= 0, returns None.
    """
    T = np.asarray(T, dtype=np.float64)
    n = (len(T) + 1) // 2
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if T[0] <= 1e-300:
        return None

    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p0 = np.sqrt(float(T[0]))
    p[0] = p0

    for k in range(1, n):
        if k > 1:
            dot = float(np.dot(p[1:k], p[k - 1:0:-1]))
        else:
            dot = 0.0
        val = (float(T[k]) - dot) / (2.0 * p0)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            return None
        p[k] = val

    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return p / s


def build_initializations(n, parent, rng):
    """Diverse non-negative initial guesses of length n."""
    inits = []
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        p = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        inits.append(p)
        inits.append(resample_sequence(parent[::-1], n))
        p2 = p * (1.0 + 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
        s2 = float(p2.sum())
        if s2 > 1e-12:
            inits.append(p2 / s2)

    inits.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.7 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    inits.append(np.maximum(0.1, 1.0 - 0.6 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)))

    # Smooth random perturbation.
    noise = rng.standard_standard if False else rng.standard_normal(n)
    try:
        freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
        cutoff = 10.0
        weights = 1.0 / (1.0 + (freq * float(n) / cutoff) ** 2)
        smooth = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(noise) * weights)
        inits.append(0.8 + 0.4 * smooth)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Geometric tail.
    if n > 20:
        r = 1.0 - 5.0 / float(n)
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        inits.append((1.0 - r) * np.power(r, t))

    # Beta-like endpoint-weighted shape.
    eps = 1.0 / float(n)
    inits.append(np.power(x + eps, -0.15) * np.power(1.0 - x + eps, -0.25))

    # Half-square-root of the ideal flat convolution.
    if 256 <= n <= 8000:
        c = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        p = poly_sqrt_from_target(c)
        if p is not None:
            inits.append(p)

    out = []
    for a in inits:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s > 1e-12 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            out.append(arr / s)
    return out


def random_perturb(a, rng, scale):
    """Multiplicative random perturbation preserving non-negativity."""
    n = len(a)
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    b = a * np.exp(scale * z)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return a.copy()
    return b / s


def gn_flatten(a_in, deadline=None, target_score=1.498, max_iter=1000000, verbose=False):
    """
    Main Gauss-Newton water-filling optimizer.

    Returns (best_sequence, best_score).
    """
    n = len(a_in)
    if n <= 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        return a, score_of(a)

    cn = 2 * n - 1
    avg = 1.0 / cn
    lb_score = 2.0 * n / cn + 1e-9

    target_sc = float(target_score)
    if not np.isfinite(target_sc) or target_sc <= lb_score:
        target_sc = lb_score

    a = normalize_sequence(a_in, 1.0)
    C = self_conv(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(C)))
    if best_sc <= lb_score + 1e-7:
        return best_a, best_sc

    relax = 1.0
    trust = 1.0
    no_improve = 0
    flipped = False
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765)

    max_a0 = float(np.max(a)) if n else 1.0
    small_floor = 1e-15 * max(1.0, max_a0)

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        a = np.maximum(a, small_floor)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 1e-12:
            break
        a = a / S

        C = self_conv(a)
        m = float(np.max(C))
        sc = float(2.0 * n * m)

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            if verbose:
                print(f"    gn n={n} it={it} score={best_sc:.10f}")

        if sc <= lb_score + 1e-7:
            break

        # Desired maximum convolution coefficient, normalized by sum(a)^2=1.
        if target_sc < sc:
            m_target = target_sc / (2.0 * n)
        else:
            m_target = m - 1e-6 * max(1e-300, m - avg)

        if m_target <= avg:
            L = avg + 1e-12 * max(1e-300, m - avg)
        elif m_target >= m:
            L = m - 1e-12 * max(1e-300, m - avg)
        else:
            L = m_target

        if m > avg:
            f_target = (L - avg) / (m - avg)
            f_target = min(1.0, max(0.0, f_target))
            # Relaxation moves target toward current extreme; target f=0 is flat.
            f_use = 1.0 - relax * (1.0 - f_target)
            f_use = min(1.0, max(0.02, f_use))
            L_use = avg + f_use * (m - avg)
        else:
            L_use = avg

        T = water_target(C, L_use)
        csum = float(C.sum())
        if csum > 0.0:
            T = T * csum

        D = T - C
        dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
        if dmax <= 1e-15 * max(1.0, m):
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                break
            continue

        E = 0.5 * D
        delta = solve_p_delta(a, E)
        mad = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
        if mad <= 1e-300:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                break
            continue

        # Precompute exact pieces for line search.
        V = cross_conv(a, delta)
        W = self_conv(delta)
        s_delta = float(delta.sum())
        maxP = float(np.max(a))

        # Positivity bound for additive update a + lambda delta.
        neg = delta < 0.0
        if np.any(neg):
            lam_pos = float(np.min(np.maximum(a, small_floor)[neg] / (-delta[neg])))
        else:
            lam_pos = 1e9

        # Trust region: lambda * max(delta) ~ O(max(a) * trust).
        scale_limit = 2.0 * trust * maxP / mad if mad > 0.0 else 1e9
        lam_max = min(lam_pos * 0.999, scale_limit, 1e6)
        if not np.isfinite(lam_max) or lam_max <= 0.0:
            lam_max = 1e-12

        candidates = []
        base_scale = min(1.0, 0.5 * maxP / mad if mad > 0.0 else 1.0, 0.999 * lam_max)
        if not np.isfinite(base_scale) or base_scale <= 0.0:
            base_scale = 1e-12 * lam_max

        for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625, 0.0078125):
            candidates.append(base_scale * f)
        for f in (0.999, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
            candidates.append(lam_max * f)

        if lam_max > 1e-300:
            lo_log = max(-30.0, float(np.log10(lam_max * 1e-8)))
            hi_log = float(np.log10(lam_max * 0.999))
            if hi_log > lo_log:
                for lg in np.linspace(lo_log, hi_log, 12):
                    candidates.append(float(10.0 ** lg))

        best_lam = 0.0
        best_try = sc

        for lam in candidates:
            if lam <= 0.0 or lam >= lam_max * 1.001:
                continue
            snew = 1.0 + lam * s_delta
            if snew <= 1e-12:
                continue
            Ctry = C + (2.0 * lam) * V + (lam * lam) * W
            maxC = float(np.max(Ctry))
            sc_try = 2.0 * n * maxC / (snew * snew)
            if sc_try < best_try:
                best_try = sc_try
                best_lam = lam

        accepted = (best_lam > 0.0) and (best_try < sc)

        if accepted:
            a_try = a + best_lam * delta
            if np.any(a_try < 0.0):
                a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
            Snew = float(a_try.sum())
            if Snew > 1e-12:
                a = a_try / Snew
                no_improve = 0

                if best_lam >= 0.9 * lam_max:
                    trust = min(1e4, trust * 3.0)
                    relax = min(1.0, relax * 1.15)
                elif best_lam < 0.2 * lam_max:
                    trust = max(1.0, trust * 0.75)
                    relax = max(0.05, relax * 0.9)

                if verbose and (it < 3 or it % 25 == 0):
                    print(f"    gn n={n} it={it} lambda={best_lam:.6e} try={best_try:.10f}")
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            trust = 1.0
            relax = max(0.05, relax * 0.75)

            # Random multiplicative escape.
            if no_improve > 20 and (no_improve % 20 == 0):
                for attempt in range(3):
                    scale = 0.01 + 0.01 * attempt
                    cand = random_perturb(a, rng, scale)
                    scand = score_of(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(scand) and scand < sc * 1.01:
                        a = cand
                        no_improve = 0
                        relax = 0.6
                        trust = 1.0
                        break

            # One exact reversal: score invariant, but changes the polynomial
            # orientation and often changes the local least-squares direction.
            if no_improve > 100 and not flipped:
                a = a[::-1].copy()
                flipped = True
                no_improve = 0
                relax = 0.6

            if no_improve > 350:
                break

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S > 1e-12:
        a = a / S
    C = self_conv(a)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(C)))
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def optimize_length(n, current_best, parent, rng, deadline, target_score, multi_start=False):
    """Run the Gauss-Newton solver for one length with several starts."""
    if n <= 0:
        return current_best.copy(), score_of(current_best)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline is not None and now >= deadline - 2.0:
        return current_best.copy(), score_of(current_best)

    candidates = []
    if current_best is not None and len(current_best) > 0:
        a_res = resample_sequence(current_best, n)
        sc = score_of(a_res)
        candidates.append((sc, a_res))

    inits = build_initializations(n, parent, rng)
    for a in inits[:12]:
        sc = score_of(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 2.5:
            candidates.append((sc, a))

    if not candidates:
        a0 = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        return a0, score_of(a0)

    candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    a0 = candidates[0][1]
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = candidates[0][0]

    phase_tl = deadline - now if deadline is not None else 0.0

    use_multi = False
    if multi_start and phase_tl > 240.0 and len(candidates) > 1:
        a1 = candidates[1][1]
        diff = float(np.linalg.norm(a0 - a1) + 1e-300)
        denom = max(1e-300, float(np.linalg.norm(a0)))
        use_multi = (diff / denom > 1e-4) and (candidates[1][0] < best_sc + 0.05)

    if use_multi:
        end1 = now + 0.6 * phase_tl
        a1, sc1 = gn_flatten(candidates[0][1], deadline=end1, target_score=target_score)
        if sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = sc1, a1

        a2, sc2 = gn_flatten(candidates[1][1], deadline=deadline, target_score=target_score)
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = sc2, a2
    else:
        a1, sc1 = gn_flatten(a0, deadline=deadline, target_score=target_score)
        if sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc, best_a = sc1, a1

    return best_a, best_sc


def smooth_obj_and_grad(x, beta, reg):
    """Smoothed log-sum-exp objective + regularization for optional L-BFGS."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    n = len(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z)
    ez_sum = float(ez.sum())
    if ez_sum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ez_sum):
        p = np.full(len(c), 1.0 / len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        logsum = 0.0
    else:
        p = ez / ez_sum
        logsum = float(np.log(ez_sum))

    Lval = cmax + logsum / beta

    cn = 2 * n - 1
    m_fft = 1 << (cn - 1).bit_length()
    RA = np.fft.rfft(a, m_fft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m_fft)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(RA) * P, m_fft)[:n]

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    fval = 2.0 * n * Lval / S2 + reg * (S - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = 2.0 * n * (2.0 * corr / S2 - 2.0 * Lval / S3)
    grad += 2.0 * reg * (S - 1.0)
    return float(fval), grad


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter=60, maxfun=200, reg=20.0):
    """Small bounded L-BFGS polish.  Returns exact (score, sequence)."""
    if minimize is None:
        return score_of(a0), a0.copy()

    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0, 1.0)
    n = len(a0)
    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1000.0))

    opts = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-11,
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 25,
        "disp": False,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_obj_and_grad,
            a0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            args=(beta, reg),
            options=opts,
        )
        a_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        a_out = a0.copy()

    a_out = np.maximum(a_out, 0.0)
    s = float(a_out.sum())
    if s > 1e-12:
        a_out = a_out / s
    return score_of(a_out), a_out


def make_valid_output(a):
    """Convert best internal sequence to a valid Python list."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.full(1024, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        a = np.full(1024, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Search over several lengths and return the best non-negative sequence found.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    work_deadline = t0 + budget - 60.0

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 32:
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20260217)

    if parent is not None:
        best_a = normalize_sequence(parent, 1.0)
        best_score = score_of(best_a)
        prog = "parent"
    else:
        n0 = 6962
        best_a = np.full(n0, 1.0 / n0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0
        prog = "uniform"

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.full(6962, 1.0 / 6962, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0
        prog = "uniform fallback"

    print(f"Initial best: score={best_score:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}, source={prog}")
    print(f"Budget={budget:.0f}s, work_deadline margin=60s")

    # Choose lengths to explore.
    base_len = len(best_a)
    raw_lengths = [base_len]
    for m in (
        int(base_len * 1.25),
        int(base_len * 1.75),
        int(base_len * 2.5),
        10240,
        14000,
        20480,
        24576,
    ):
        if 2048 <= m <= 32768:
            raw_lengths.append(m)

    lengths = []
    seen = set()
    for m in raw_lengths:
        m = int(m)
        if m not in seen:
            seen.add(m)
            lengths.append(m)
    lengths = lengths[:5]

    fractions = [0.40, 0.18, 0.15, 0.12, 0.10]
    target_score = 1.498

    for idx, n in enumerate(lengths):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = work_deadline - now
        if remaining <= 90.0:
            break

        frac = fractions[idx] if idx < len(fractions) else 0.08
        phase_time = frac * (work_deadline - t0)
        phase_deadline = min(work_deadline, now + phase_time)
        if phase_deadline <= now + 30.0:
            continue

        print(f"\nLength phase: n={n}, phase_time={phase_time:.0f}s, current best={best_score:.10f}")
        multi = False if n == base_len else True
        cand_a, cand_sc = optimize_length(
            n,
            best_a,
            parent,
            rng,
            deadline=phase_deadline,
            target_score=target_score,
            multi_start=multi,
        )
        if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < best_score:
            best_a = cand_a
            best_score = cand_sc
            print(f"  NEW BEST from n={n}: score={best_score:.10f}")
        else:
            print(f"  no improvement from n={n}; best stays {best_score:.10f}")

    # Final short Gauss-Newton polish on the global best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = work_deadline - now
    if remaining > 180.0:
        print(f"\nFinal gn polish: remaining={remaining:.0f}s")
        end = min(work_deadline, now + 0.6 * remaining)
        cand_a, cand_sc = gn_flatten(
            best_a,
            deadline=end,
            target_score=max(1.0, best_score - 0.001) if best_score > 1.0 else 1.0,
            verbose=False,
        )
        if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < best_score:
            best_a = cand_a
            best_score = cand_sc
            print(f"  polish NEW BEST: {best_score:.10f}")
        else:
            print(f"  polish no improvement: {best_score:.10f}")

    # Optional bounded L-BFGS polish, guarded by time and length.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = work_deadline - now
    if minimize is not None and remaining > 300.0 and 256 <= len(best_a) <= 24576:
        beta = 120.0 if len(best_a) > 12000 else 260.0
        print(f"\nL-BFGS polish (beta={beta:.0f}, maxfun=200): remaining={remaining:.0f}s")
        cand_a, cand_sc = run_lbfgs(best_a, beta=beta, maxiter=70, maxfun=220, reg=20.0)
        if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < best_score:
            best_a = cand_a
            best_score = cand_sc
            print(f"  L-BFGS NEW BEST: {best_score:.10f}")
        else:
            print(f"  L-BFGS no improvement: {best_score:.10f}")

    # Cheap random scatter in the final seconds.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = work_deadline - now
    if remaining > 30.0:
        rng2 = np.random.default_rng(71713)
        scales = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.04)
        count = max(5, int(remaining // 2))
        for i in range(count):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > work_deadline - 15.0:
                break
            s = scales[i % len(scales)]
            cand = random_perturb(best_a, rng2, s)
            sc = score_of(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_a = cand
                best_score = sc
                print(f"  scatter NEW BEST at i={i}: {best_score:.10f}")

    # Final internal check.
    best_a = normalize_sequence(best_a, 1.0)
    final_internal = score_of(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(final_internal) and final_internal < best_score:
        best_score = final_internal

    out = make_valid_output(best_a)

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, length={len(out)}")

    if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified > 1e100:
        out = make_valid_output(np.full(1024, 1.0, dtype=np.float64))
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            print(f"Fallback verified={verified:.10f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    return out
```
